Mouse Wheel Delay
Set the minimum time in milliseconds between consecutive mouse wheel inputs before another signal is sent.\n\nLow values will make it easier to trigger an action twice in quick succession if it is bound to the mouse wheel. This can also make it more likely to accidentally trigger the action more times than intended.\n\nNote:^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^ By default, scrolling the mouse wheel is used to switch weapons.

Ray Tracing
^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^Ray tracing^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ is a rendering technique that simulates realistic lighting and visual effects in games by tracing the path of light through a scene. It offers more ^?#x0df01504d5eb8601e.#x02f30da4ea89649a^accurate reflections, shadows and ambient occlusion^?#x0d0fc60310d4a2ef9.#x0871f5414ac580f9^ compared to traditional rasterization methods.\n\nNote: ^?#x08e606b1af56d8ac2.#x070743ccf32890e6c^This settings only have effects in multiplayer and zombies maps.
